All this is an argument attempting to refute those who refuse to accept the denial of outer objects."

And in response, Master Vinitadeva says \"Actions and their objects are like an injury in a dream.\" He is saying that, even though the pus and blood do not exist as outer objects, nonetheless they can perform the function of being something to eat or drink. He proves his point by using a great many examples, such as a dream."

If the color blue were to exist as an external object, then the following would occur when this color appeared directly to a sense perception grasping blue; that is, with such a perception found in the mental stream of one of those who \"only sees this side\" [which is another name for those who have not yet perceived emptiness directly]. When an earlier instance of the perception of blue ends, what actually happens is that it plants a mental seed which eventually grows into a later instance of the same perception of blue, when the seed ripens. Suppose the blue were not just this kind of appearance, but rather an appearance where blue as an outer object were transmitting a likeness of itself and thereby appearing to one's perceptions. The three different objects would be appearing to them because each of the objects was transmitting a likeness of itself to their perception. All of this would be happening independent of any process where each being's karma planted a mental seed, which later ripened and produced the appearance of the object."

Therefore what happened was that, at first, he saw some water. Later on, he was forced by his karma to stop seeing water and saw only bare, parched earth. Then he had an impression where he thought the water had dried up. The case with the fruit tree is the same. Although at first the craving spirit sees a tree loaded with fruit, later on his karma forces him to stop seeing fruit, and all he sees is bare branches. Then he has an impression where he thinks that the tree has no fruit any more."

===== root-review-targets.md =====
# Withheld preparation leads: C05:289–291

This is source preparation, not a proposal, baseline, erratum or approval.
Original readers must not read these leads before both angle originals freeze.
The primary reader must disclose its own prior preparation if it later authors
an original. Every correspondence still needs independent proposal,
reconciliation and separate semantic review.

The complete argument at283–297 was read in Wylie and English. Selected289–291
ACIP was read in full; all six complete physical C05 extents match under ASCII
whitespace folding. The original file remains untouched. The captured spine,
master and corpus hashes identify the actual immutable input snapshots.
All96 complete result sets were reopened and compared exactly to SQLite and the
original master/corpus objects. This includes72 initial lexical queries,
11 additional lexical queries,9 equality queries and4 direct parallel queries.
All HGM gloss variants, source tiers and statuses were read, including positive
wider equivalents and unglossed entries. Full raw objects remain in the files.

## 289: the consequence of an external-object hypothesis

The first conditional appearance, the three beings' individual karma, planted
seed and ripening lead to the second appearance predicate. English brings the
objects forward before the source's first explicit dngos po. Do not align
repeated objects/three/appearing/beings by simple left-to-right string order:
the source's first gsum po qualifies beings, its next gsum po qualifies objects,
and gsum ga is the later all-three claim. Both snang predicates have English
appearing; the source rtogs then ma rtogs contrasts positive perceiving with
elliptical do not. Resolve all occurrences and ownership from the argument.

Positive HTG7486 thun mong ma yin pa contains specific and different; HTG23232
the inflected form has unique/not shared. Keep the whole established unit if
defensible; never detach ma and invent a second English negation there.
HTG8435 de ltar explicitly includes this were how, while na supplies the
conditional. d5 versus full grammatical d3 still requires judgment.
HTG12290 bag chags licenses seed; smin pa14839 ripened. The unrelated mined
bzhag pa16516/nothing but and bzhag16513/three diamonds do not license the
actual planted correspondence by glossary lookup alone. Use the actual source
and HGM sentence, acknowledging these positive but mismatching mined rows.

ma yin pas has no exact entry, but ma yin13878 has would not be/not and many
other real equivalents. An inflection-level zero is not source absence.
gang zag1690/1740 includes beings, re res25320 each one, rtogs6756 perceiving,
dgos pa2436 would have to be. Distributed mind/later/collected framing must not
be captured merely to fill English. The final bkag pa/argument-to-refute has
expanded context about refusal and outer objects; the latter concepts are
explicit in288 but not separate words in289. Do not manufacture exponents.

## 290: explaining Vinitadeva's quotation

Vinitadeva's name and Master are contextual expansion of lung de'i and the
preceding quotation, not newly present Tibetan proper-name tokens. HTG19435
lung supports quotation and HTG8617 don explicitly supports real meaning;
the don query also returns18170 yul as a variant, so preserve both records.
des na8600 therefore is separate from that content.

Two complete phyi rol don du grub pa relations and repeated existed/exist/as
need exact role/occurrence checks. phyi rol don11899 is a positive established
external/outer object unit; the whole inflected grammatical sequence has no
exact entry. grub pa2046 includes existed/exist. Neither zero lookup justifies
omission by itself nor a broad phrase permits supplied English ownership.

med na24656 has positive if there was not and longer conditional-negation
equivalents; actual English uses Suppose and didn't exist. ga la yod1644 has
the full how then could there exist phrase. Keep lexical/grammatical depth,
the hypothetical negation and rhetorical question distinct. thigs pa7427/drop,
rnag10448/pus, gang ba1680/full/whole and klung314/river offer tighter coverage.
Positive rnag gis gang ba10450/full of pus is a real full entry, but its
phrase/compound eligibility still needs independent judgment. The final pus
belongs to mthong/see; the earlier two occurrences belong to drop and river.

'on kyang17066/though and dbang gis13091/forced to do so by are attested,
with the latter a grammatical cause/force phrase. sogs20919/and so on needs
the exact source-specific allowance if retained as d5, not an exception that
permits unrelated English heads. bstan pa's reporting force is distributed
across the explanation; don't force a final English say that is not there.

## 291: function despite absence as an external object

der thal23514 has the complete And this is certainly the case as a positive
variant; it is a grammatical consequence statement rather than automatically
a d5 dictionary unit. 'og tu17008/later corresponds to the later-text setup.
Source rtsod pa/argues and lan du/in response are distinct. Master Vinitadeva
is contextual English expansion again; do not create a proper-name span.

The first rnag khrag has explicit pus and blood. The second English pus and
blood is an anaphoric reprise under de; no second Tibetan compound exists.
The actual first compound permits two strictly narrower consecutive d7 members
if their specific ownership is defensible. Positive reciprocal glossary
variants rnag10448 and khrag1368 remain evidence, not a reason to reverse the
clear current reading or to hide the variants.

First ma grub/did not exist contrasts with later ma grub/do not exist. Do not
confuse the two. sems tsam20770 positively contains only a part of the mind;
bdag nyid9092 includes itself. Wider positive wording does not automatically
establish compound boundaries or license every supplied word at d5.
mi nus24575 has could never; the second positive nus pa10227/can is distinct.
bza' btung25002 has both eat or drink and something to eat or drink. Both
occurrences must be individually resolved; bza'16908/eat and btung6535/drink
are candidate tighter members only inside the actual chosen parent.

The quoted bya byed12538 explicitly has Actions and their objects; rmi lam14790
dream, gnod pa10388 injury and 'dra9726 like provide the remaining quoted
content. Supplied articles/prepositions must be independently justified or
left unwrapped. The second rmi lam is the final example. byed12829/perform,
bya ba12506/function and dpe11195/examples are useful lexical targets.
du mas23393 includes the whole by using a great many, while du ma8270 has
a great many: source grammar and exact English boundaries determine d5/d3,
not the mere existence of the wider glossary phrase. sgrub par3245 is present
but unglossed; sgrub3221/proving and sgrub pa3229/proves are positive. Preserve
the source's whole expression and honorific mdzad pa; do not infer no English
counterpart from the inflected row's null hgm_gloss.

## Parallel evidence and limits

C15:434/435 are identical to289/290 in all three fields. P7:34 matches290
English exactly but has DWAGS/DVAGS and punctuation differences in Tibetan.
C15:436 matches291 ACIP/Wylie, while English differs only in two punctuation
replacements: comma-space→double hyphen at76:78 and comma→double hyphen
at178:179 in C05 coordinates (584 versus585 characters). Full raw rows and
complete difference records are saved. This establishes digital comparison,
not independent publication or ingestion lineage. No erratum is proposed.

A read-only exploratory metadata-table probe failed because that table does
not exist; the actual source schema was then read directly. The probe changed
no input and was not part of a canonical generator. Full query outputs that
initially exceeded the display limit were reread in bounded chunks; no missing
output is treated as inspected. Capture helper completed first run EXIT0.

===== tibetan/289/report.md =====
C05:289 — Tibetan-first independent proposal; Codex; PROVISIONAL, no semantic approval or hgm_gloss change.
Source: full spine row 1671; all six selected fields and all 15 context rows verified, plus original corpus records and whitespace-normalized physical ACIP/English. See ../evidence/snapshot-verification.json and original-corpus-verification.json.
The first objects/three in English belong to later Tibetan dngos po/gsum po; earlier 'gro ba/gsum po belong to the three beings. All intended occurrences equal canonical ranges.
thun mong ma yin pa → specific and different is an established HGM compound; bzhag pa → planted is justified by the complete seed/karma construction, despite unrelated auto-aligned master glosses.
Challenge first: the anticipatory objects placement. ma rtogs → do not is a phrase with an anaphoric pro-verb; brten nas and the final expanded target remain omitted for distributed correspondence.
The single null is kyis: active causal subject structure realizes the agentive relation, without a separate case word.
Evidence: material-evidence-map.json maps each span to full originals, current master entries and all unwrapped intervals; decisions.json records roles, omissions and exact intended ranges. Shared complete queries/rows appear once in ../evidence/; exact hashes are in ../freeze.json.
Errata: 0. Registered apparatus/cap/escape/footnote classes checked; no source defect supported. C15:434 matches ACIP/Wylie/English.
Parallel text identity does not establish independent publication or an ingestion lineage; shared extents and differences are recorded in ../evidence/parallel-comparisons.json.
EXIT=0; STDERR_BYTES=0; BODY_BYTES=4409; SPANS=24; D5=19; NULLS=1; ERRATA=0. Actual native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit: attempts/01/; proof.json verifies exact saved body bytes and unwrapped full source fields.

===== english/289/report.md =====
C05:289 — independent English-first proposal by Codex; PROVISIONAL, no hgm_gloss change.
Source baseline: 129e86afae8431aacfb1ee1cee65d8635dd265b8 through285 only; no claim to a through288 landing baseline.
Most challengeable: dngos po gsum in the process clause is expressed as the opening English three objects, then resumed by they; eng_order preserves this fronting and the later all-three consequence.
Retained thun mong ma yin pa → specific and different, bag chags → seed, bzhag pa → planted; the unrelated exact dictionary bzhag/bzhag pa glosses were checked, and source/corpus C05:215 supplies the planted usage.
Omitted de ltar and distributed syntax rather than claiming this were how; final rtogs pa is a justified lexical ellipsis null under they do not.
C15:434 matches ACIP, Wylie and English. Original ASCII target text matches after whitespace collapse. No registered-class or new spelling/English defect found.
Counts: 26 spans; 1 null; 20 d5 (19 non-null); 0 errata.
Native run: 289 EXIT=0 stdout_bytes=4477 stderr_bytes=0
Exact native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit: 289/native-attempts/attempt-01/; body equals stdout, both plain texts equal source, and every actual range equals its intended occurrence (verification.json).
Body SHA-256: 74486ce92ef9394d188d2a33204bbd7a4fac591780caf2f0f94ec1cf7b660bfb; spec SHA-256: 62beb19bb14f8ac5731380a4e4f813d4509e1123f2a63bc9371c59e5e7564389.
Complete source/query evidence is shared once in ../shared-evidence-index.json (SHA-256 6133655de762810e992396fdc26afd379de290c183e867ca1d997c56364a1ebd); exact span evidence, source roles and omissions are in decisions.json.
All superseded authoring/helper failures are preserved in ../tooling-history.json; final files are covered by ../freeze-manifest.json. Mechanical checks do not imply semantic approval.

===== reconciled/289/report.md =====
C05:289 — reconciled by Codex (GPT-6 Astra); PROVISIONAL. No hgm_gloss or original/source changes.
Source/governing origin is immutable129e86afae8431aacfb1ee1cee65d8635dd265b8 through285; later through288 acceptance/landing is a separate root responsibility.
Both complete frozen originals were considered: 50 original spans have exact angle/id/depth/text/ranges, original reasons and final IDs in original-dispositions.json.
Six actual original canonical replays were independently hash-checked and reused as explicitly authorized; evidence/identity-and-original-replay-reuse.json pins the native streams and all164 frozen original files.
de ltar/this were how at d3: check that the complete manner recast is licensed by de+ltar without moving If away from na.
la brten nas/through retains the dependency relation; ma rtogs/do not is an anaphoric phrase rather than a lexical rtogs absence claim.
First dngos po/gsum maps to the opening English objects/three; the earlier source numeral belongs to beings, and final gsum ga has its own all-three members.
kyis is a grammatical-fusion null in the active karma/planted relation, not uncertain content hidden as absent.
Complete final ordered seven-key tuples and every actual intended occurrence are in final-ordered-tuples.json; cls/nul remain in spec.json.
Material omissions, positive wider-variant decisions and all unwrapped source intervals are in material-omissions.json. Source/lexical objects and complete query sets are shared once in ../evidence/.
All source suspicions were checked against all five refutation grounds; ../source-suspicion-five-ground-checks.json records quotes, original physical/archive text, counts, class checks and defensibility. errata.json is empty.
C15 textual equality and P7/C15 differences establish shared digital extent only, not independent publication or ingestion lineage.
EXIT=0; STDOUT_BYTES=5091; STDERR_BYTES=0; SPANS=28; NULLS=1; ERRATA=0.
Actual final native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit and runtime identities: attempts/01/; body.html is byte-identical stdout.
SPEC_BYTES=4946; SPEC_SHA256=0683ffadb7fadfb3fb94e2ff7a12070509ea0c46ab5d5dbc95f1be2aad069f07.
BODY_BYTES=5091; BODY_SHA256=5fd5a7eca34fc2491ea9f5be8bf8074b33e80719b57a683220dee3c5ce53af5b.
Mechanical verification confirms complete plain-source fidelity, every wrapped token, member shape, occurrence and eng_order. It is not separate semantic approval.
A metadata-only off-by-one intended range for290/by was corrected to [396,398]; the already correct native body/spec were unchanged. Prior helper and metadata snapshot are preserved in ../history/.
Fresh separate skeptical semantic review is required before acceptance and landing; no acceptance, approval, commit or review waiver is claimed.

===== tibetan/290/report.md =====
C05:290 — Tibetan-first independent proposal; Codex; PROVISIONAL, no semantic approval or hgm_gloss change.
Source: full spine row 1672; all six selected fields and all 15 context rows verified, plus original corpus records and whitespace-normalized physical ACIP/English. See ../evidence/snapshot-verification.json and original-corpus-verification.json.
First grub pa attaches to existed occurrence2 after craving spirit; second grub pa attaches to occurrence3. The unclaimed first existed is GMR's duplicate explanation.
Challenge first: 'ga' yang → single is an indefinite minimizer under med, not an inferred gcig. med → didn't exist supplies the negative polarity; the article stays unwrapped.
lta ga la yod → How then could there exist is a complete rhetorical phrase. Prior passing ga la yod-only version and native output remain in superseded/01-before-rhetorical-phrase-completion and attempts/01.
bstan pa and ji ltar...ltar gyi remain omitted as distributed explanation/comparison; no nulls are used.
Evidence: material-evidence-map.json maps each span to full originals, current master entries and all unwrapped intervals; decisions.json records roles, omissions and exact intended ranges. Shared complete queries/rows appear once in ../evidence/; exact hashes are in ../freeze.json.
Errata: 0. Registered apparatus/cap/escape/footnote classes checked; no source defect supported. C15:435 matches ACIP/Wylie/English; P7:34 matches English with Tibetan differences.
Parallel text identity does not establish independent publication or an ingestion lineage; shared extents and differences are recorded in ../evidence/parallel-comparisons.json.
EXIT=0; STDERR_BYTES=0; BODY_BYTES=4577; SPANS=26; D5=21; NULLS=0; ERRATA=0. Actual native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit: attempts/02/; proof.json verifies exact saved body bytes and unwrapped full source fields.

===== english/290/report.md =====
C05:290 — independent English-first proposal by Codex; PROVISIONAL, no hgm_gloss change.
Source baseline: 129e86afae8431aacfb1ee1cee65d8635dd265b8 through285 only; no claim to a through288 landing baseline.
Most challengeable: dbang → forced is a nominal-to-verbal recast, corroborated by C05:295. The first grub pa pairs with the second English existed in the explicit external-object paraphrase.
All three pus and both outer object occurrences are explicit. ga la → How then belongs to the river question; ji ltar is omitted in its separate correlative.
Omitted explanatory repeated existence/reporting wording; no nulls. sogs → and so on is a licensed closing gesture; landing would require its usual named sogs allowlist entry.
C15:435 matches all three text fields; P7:34 matches English but differs in ACIP/Wylie. No heading glue/cap or new erratum; lineage remains unproved.
Counts: 33 spans; 0 null; 24 d5 (24 non-null); 0 errata.
Native run: 290 EXIT=0 stdout_bytes=4998 stderr_bytes=0
Exact native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit: 290/native-attempts/attempt-01/; body equals stdout, both plain texts equal source, and every actual range equals its intended occurrence (verification.json).
Body SHA-256: 79f3a6b21f54c6ab77bbb46a6c68cb5be8c778d0c0868f1d0aa675a9d10d21c0; spec SHA-256: f1195dab3f021629f9a72373fee24e3441cada1b5965a15395bc06077eeed2b4.
Complete source/query evidence is shared once in ../shared-evidence-index.json (SHA-256 6133655de762810e992396fdc26afd379de290c183e867ca1d997c56364a1ebd); exact span evidence, source roles and omissions are in decisions.json.
All superseded authoring/helper failures are preserved in ../tooling-history.json; final files are covered by ../freeze-manifest.json. Mechanical checks do not imply semantic approval.

===== reconciled/290/report.md =====
C05:290 — reconciled by Codex (GPT-6 Astra); PROVISIONAL. No hgm_gloss or original/source changes.
Source/governing origin is immutable129e86afae8431aacfb1ee1cee65d8635dd265b8 through285; later through288 acceptance/landing is a separate root responsibility.
Both complete frozen originals were considered: 59 original spans have exact angle/id/depth/text/ranges, original reasons and final IDs in original-dispositions.json.
Six actual original canonical replays were independently hash-checked and reused as explicitly authorized; evidence/identity-and-original-replay-reuse.json pins the native streams and all164 frozen original files.
don/meaning is the tighter boundary despite positive real meaning evidence; 'ga'/single similarly respects its own positive entry while leaving emphatic yang unclaimed.
lta ga la yod stays whole at d3, with the complete rhetorical modal/existence recast.
Final gis/by transfers the causal role of las kyi dbang into the English forced by their karma construction; do not confuse it with rnag gis.
sogs is d5 with exact allowance c5p97/s290w23; lowering depth to avoid the supplied-head gate is prohibited.
Complete final ordered seven-key tuples and every actual intended occurrence are in final-ordered-tuples.json; cls/nul remain in spec.json.
Material omissions, positive wider-variant decisions and all unwrapped source intervals are in material-omissions.json. Source/lexical objects and complete query sets are shared once in ../evidence/.
All source suspicions were checked against all five refutation grounds; ../source-suspicion-five-ground-checks.json records quotes, original physical/archive text, counts, class checks and defensibility. errata.json is empty.
C15 textual equality and P7/C15 differences establish shared digital extent only, not independent publication or ingestion lineage.
EXIT=0; STDOUT_BYTES=5618; STDERR_BYTES=0; SPANS=33; NULLS=0; ERRATA=0.
Actual final native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit and runtime identities: attempts/01/; body.html is byte-identical stdout.
SPEC_BYTES=5575; SPEC_SHA256=801ee49f256669317e701901cbb92422f20270819929dc2b88e477485c9223af.
BODY_BYTES=5618; BODY_SHA256=2886e6d6f1a8925fba026abc42ef1d1050dc4cc37d25abcfae46948d41aef03c.
Mechanical verification confirms complete plain-source fidelity, every wrapped token, member shape, occurrence and eng_order. It is not separate semantic approval.
A metadata-only off-by-one intended range for290/by was corrected to [396,398]; the already correct native body/spec were unchanged. Prior helper and metadata snapshot are preserved in ../history/.
Fresh separate skeptical semantic review is required before acceptance and landing; no acceptance, approval, commit or review waiver is claimed.

===== tibetan/291/report.md =====
C05:291 — Tibetan-first independent proposal; Codex; PROVISIONAL, no semantic approval or hgm_gloss change.
Source: full spine row 1673; all six selected fields and all 15 context rows verified, plus original corpus records and whitespace-normalized physical ACIP/English. See ../evidence/snapshot-verification.json and original-corpus-verification.json.
The two dreams, outer-object clauses, function phrases and negative exist predicates have separate verified occurrences. rnag khrag owns only the first pus and blood; the second names the referent of de.
Challenge first: bya byed → Actions and their objects is kept whole on explicit HGM compound evidence; no byed → objects member is invented. bza' btung retains the nominal HGM gloss something to eat or drink twice.
bdag nyid is omitted because part...itself distributes the nature expression. The one na attaches to first if, which governs both clauses; repeated if remains unwrapped.
The single null is mdzad pa: honorific light-verb support is fused into proves; no separate doing/honorific exponent is asserted.
Evidence: material-evidence-map.json maps each span to full originals, current master entries and all unwrapped intervals; decisions.json records roles, omissions and exact intended ranges. Shared complete queries/rows appear once in ../evidence/; exact hashes are in ../freeze.json.
Errata: 0. Registered apparatus/cap/escape/footnote classes checked; no source defect supported. C15:436 matches ACIP/Wylie; English differs only in two punctuation locations. Quoted verse lineation is retained.
Parallel text identity does not establish independent publication or an ingestion lineage; shared extents and differences are recorded in ../evidence/parallel-comparisons.json.
EXIT=0; STDERR_BYTES=0; BODY_BYTES=5905; SPANS=35; D5=26; NULLS=1; ERRATA=0. Actual native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit: attempts/01/; proof.json verifies exact saved body bytes and unwrapped full source fields.
SPEC_SHA256=26cacb4ffe585135d44a38ee96c9af3a4b8e90b7bfa79e69f844033a1a6bc32b; BODY_SHA256=948fbf49864d43d05c428965614e2aa236521ec0b641a331cf266a06e0d31c41.

===== english/291/report.md =====
C05:291 — independent English-first proposal by Codex; PROVISIONAL, no hgm_gloss change.
Source baseline: 129e86afae8431aacfb1ee1cee65d8635dd265b8 through285 only; no claim to a through288 landing baseline.
Most challengeable: bya byed → Actions and their objects stays whole, supported by HGM lexical evidence and C12:298; no speculative action/object members.
Both exist/function/eat or drink/dream occurrences are resolved. Only the reply has byed → perform; objection provide is supplied. cing attaches to the second lowercase and, outside the quote.
Omitted bdag nyid as distributed over part/itself, without a false null. mdzad is a justified honorific-light-verb null; the second pus and blood expands de and remains unwrapped.
C15:436 matches ACIP and Wylie but changes English comma/dash punctuation. C05 and its original ASCII text remain intact; no new erratum proposed.
Counts: 47 spans; 1 null; 25 d5 (24 non-null); 0 errata.
Native run: 291 EXIT=0 stdout_bytes=6842 stderr_bytes=0
Exact native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit: 291/native-attempts/attempt-01/; body equals stdout, both plain texts equal source, and every actual range equals its intended occurrence (verification.json).
Body SHA-256: b7129147b7ebf6575f241a1f9a5cabbd0612c6fabdcb6abebfbc08709cbb756d; spec SHA-256: 10302d2acd07a8536b0b7de03c60788b8577ee4c676c5253a6049eb76cb25bf1.
Complete source/query evidence is shared once in ../shared-evidence-index.json (SHA-256 6133655de762810e992396fdc26afd379de290c183e867ca1d997c56364a1ebd); exact span evidence, source roles and omissions are in decisions.json.
All superseded authoring/helper failures are preserved in ../tooling-history.json; final files are covered by ../freeze-manifest.json. Mechanical checks do not imply semantic approval.

===== reconciled/291/report.md =====
C05:291 — reconciled by Codex (GPT-6 Astra); PROVISIONAL. No hgm_gloss or original/source changes.
Source/governing origin is immutable129e86afae8431aacfb1ee1cee65d8635dd265b8 through285; later through288 acceptance/landing is a separate root responsibility.
Both complete frozen originals were considered: 82 original spans have exact angle/id/depth/text/ranges, original reasons and final IDs in original-dispositions.json.
Six actual original canonical replays were independently hash-checked and reused as explicitly authorized; evidence/identity-and-original-replay-reuse.json pins the native streams and all164 frozen original files.
bza' btung/eat or drink is tighter than the positive something to eat or drink variant; its members must remain local to each parent.
mi/never plus nus/could decomposes the explicit negative/capacity construction; positive mi nus/could never is considered and not dismissed.
bya byed/Actions and their objects is a whole technical compound, never byed/objects atomization.
des/they is an overt licensed demonstrative; final phyir/for explains der thal. Check mdzad's grammatical fusion and all repeated negation/existence/dream occurrences.
Complete final ordered seven-key tuples and every actual intended occurrence are in final-ordered-tuples.json; cls/nul remain in spec.json.
Material omissions, positive wider-variant decisions and all unwrapped source intervals are in material-omissions.json. Source/lexical objects and complete query sets are shared once in ../evidence/.
All source suspicions were checked against all five refutation grounds; ../source-suspicion-five-ground-checks.json records quotes, original physical/archive text, counts, class checks and defensibility. errata.json is empty.
C15 textual equality and P7/C15 differences establish shared digital extent only, not independent publication or ingestion lineage.
EXIT=0; STDOUT_BYTES=7626; STDERR_BYTES=0; SPANS=48; NULLS=1; ERRATA=0.
Actual final native stdin/stdout/stderr/exit and runtime identities: attempts/01/; body.html is byte-identical stdout.
SPEC_BYTES=7712; SPEC_SHA256=2dade5255fb440be38677b33f843f8c7e51cbea0068e30656b9470c1ceecccb2.
BODY_BYTES=7626; BODY_SHA256=f2ec696448456e8c3ce51bcb8bef130d96f7f6bd3d8cdb2524e9a69e07423f1c.
Mechanical verification confirms complete plain-source fidelity, every wrapped token, member shape, occurrence and eng_order. It is not separate semantic approval.
A metadata-only off-by-one intended range for290/by was corrected to [396,398]; the already correct native body/spec were unchanged. Prior helper and metadata snapshot are preserved in ../history/.
Fresh separate skeptical semantic review is required before acceptance and landing; no acceptance, approval, commit or review waiver is claimed.
